### Part38 corrective peer-review update — intentional `{}` clear semantics

**New CS02 public source evidence changes the compatibility policy.** [Review #204](https://github.com/CosmicCuddle/N-Questie/pull/204#issuecomment-6445578966) identified **2,265 historical intentional empty-table `{}` Correction clears** on simple numeric compound fields, 23 of them standalone pair fields. ## How to read and update this checklist

- **[x] VERIFIED** means the *narrow action described* is evidenced by a linked source/commit/test. It does **not** mean its parent phase, feature, release or server testing is finished.
- **[ ] ACTIVE / BLOCKED / NEXT / FUTURE** remains unchecked. **Green CI is not peer sign-off; peer PASS is not a maintainer policy decision or merge approval.**
- Verify **current PR head SHA and exact-head CI** before claiming completion; a new commit supersedes its parent's CI/review. Do not close a workstream simply because a research PR passed.
- Whenever an independent subproject starts, completes, gets blocked or gains an owner decision: **add/update a row here**, add its exact GitHub evidence, and update the existing CS02 queue only if an independently actionable review task is actually needed.
- Keep `335` untouched unless the owner separately approves a reviewed integration. Draft branches are not installs or releases. No real private server exports/identifiers in public GitHub issues, commits, CI or PR bodies; only synthetic fixtures and safe aggregates.
- When installing or testing on the WoW client is eventually approved: **back up the N-Questie addon folder and SavedVariables first**, document a rollback path, then record the separate in-game results.

## D. Eight unanswered Part 3 owner decisions — do NOT silently choose

These questions and their dependency ordering are fully presented in [draft #87 decision sheet](https://github.com/CosmicCuddle/N-Questie/pull/87). Current summary, **all UNSELECTED**:

| ID | Owner must decide |
| --- | --- |
| **B1** | Resource/output caps besides approved K64 per creature/object/item and frozen 16-MiB v2 input; proposals for combined-other 2,048, nested 4,096, 192 findings, 1,024 text/reference bytes, 256-KiB per-quest, 64-MiB bundle **are not approved** |
| **D5** | Whether to explicitly amend #42's owner-approved step 10 (`M23Batch.Run` unchanged) to permit a new **global, zero-M1 preflight worker**; frozen Part 2 can run earlier M1 calls before refusing a later missing selected ID |
| **D6** | Which of the **existing 14 fixed rejection codes** applies to an otherwise structurally valid but over-limit selected record; no invented fifteenth code |
| **R1** | Exact nested resource-entry counting formula |
| **R2** | How `spawnZones` are counted (reviewed A/B/C alternatives or owner alternative) |
| **R3** | Whether resource gates count M1-unsupported but frozen-v2-valid selected quests |
| **C1** | Complete canonical private JSON/framing/integrity rules and **encoded byte** accounting; raw M1 lengths alone are insufficient |
| **P1** | Provenance verification, trust binding and measuring required `leftoverTempCount`; prehash snapshot cannot certify post-publication cleanup |

**Approved limits must not be confused with proposals.** Approving a design review does not authorise G3 work, private export, a trusted publisher or release. If the maintainer responds, record their exact answer and link before ticking any resolved decision.

## G. Remaining completion stages — planning estimate, not release approval

**Estimated milestone groups as of 18 October 2026:** **3 for a deliberately limited first usable release**, or **7 for the full planned quest/import/validation/marker/correction scope**. These are **groups of gates**, not seven remaining commits, fixed durations or a guarantee that all features are nearly finished. Some review work can progress in parallel. No release scope has yet been selected or authorised by the maintainer.

### Limited first release — 3 gates (only if maintainer selects a limited scope)

| Gate | What is needed | Current evidence / blocker |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **L1 — scope and independent integration review** | Agree exactly which existing quest-overlay features and reviewed corrections would be in the first package; reconcile historical #2/#3 and other draft ancestors, source permissions, CS02 reviews and a reviewed trial against current `335` | #97–#101 each have 6/6 CI at recorded exact heads, **but peer review remains queued** and their green individual test branches are not one reviewed addon package |
| **L2 — safe in-game release-candidate QA** | With explicit owner permission, back up installed addon folder and both Questie SavedVariables, prepare rollback, test eligibility/minimap/intermediate counters/cache/relog/locale/phase for features **actually included**; retain 14 Oct quest-2001 starter→objective→completion→hand-in marker PASS | The basic quest-2001 lifecycle is **already verified**; several distinct manual acceptance axes and any later correction effects remain unverified |
| **L3 — packaging, maintainer acceptance and release** | Reviewed QA-artifact sharing/permission policy (not only tagged releases); New reviewed 3.3.5a-specific package manifest/build and explicit `v*` publish guard; asset-by-asset licence/credit review (including whatever Krowi/icons actually ships) and exact-source review, fresh combined CI and 3.3.5a client smoke tests, owner-approved protected-branch integration and revision/release tag, then rollback-ready deployment; N-Addon-Collection separately optional | No owner integration/release permission, release or tag has yet been granted; draft CI is **not** a distributable build |

**Limited scope means later M2.3 private engine/M2.4/M2.5 capabilities stay unfinished and cannot be marketed as included.** Even the limited release is **not ready now**; L1–L3 are completion gates.

### Full planned roadmap — 7 milestone groups

| Gate | Remaining goal | Latest source-backed status / decision owner |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **F1 — historical integration and peer-review closure** | Reconcile older quest/import/correction PRs and all applicable independent source/test reviews; preserve existing 335/14-Oct QA facts | ACTIVE; [single CS02 queue](https://github.com/CosmicCuddle/N-Questie/pull/42#issuecomment-6248354348) currently 11 done / 20 queued, reviewer resting |
| **F2 — finish approved M2.2 export/import foundations** | Reconcile staged A1/A2 export contracts, deterministic full-DB/round-trip regression, fail-closed safe writes and quest import provenance, including any actual approved release integration | FOUNDATION partially researched/tested; production and integration acceptance not inferred from synthetic CI; no new private export approved |
| **F3 — resolve eight owner-only M2.3 Part 3 contract choices** | B1, D5, D6, R1, R2, R3, C1, P1; do **not** substitute proposed caps for approved K=64 per creature/object/item and 16-MiB input | **BLOCKED on maintainer answers**; [draft decision sheet #87](https://github.com/CosmicCuddle/N-Questie/pull/87) |
| **F4 — separately approve and implement G2 → G3 validation** | Once decisions are explicit, create independently reviewed synthetic red tests (G2); only then authorise a separate pure worker/codec/provenance/trusted publisher (G3) with whole-batch refusal and unchanged frozen M0/M1/v1 | BLOCKED; this group contains **multiple separately reviewed subgates**, not one coding step |
| **F5 — independently validate private server evidence** | Real source-selection/provenance and fresh private 1203 pilot, then a small, individually reviewed item/NPC/GO batch; keep complete private rows/reports off GitHub | FUTURE after approved tooling; public synthetic Lua 5.1 passes do **not** prove that the private generated Lua did |
| **F6 — M2.4 marker and M2.5 correction rounds** | Source-verified coordinates, active spawn/zone/phase checks, text/tooltip distinctions and only individually approved quest/objective corrections | Design/research drafts exist; runtime correctness and broad client QA unproven |
| **F7 — complete in-game acceptance, integration and release** | Reviewed client-30300 `Questie-335.toc` package manifest, protection against inherited `v*` publish trigger, scoped client regression, backups/rollback, **release-asset licence/attribution inventory**, independent head-verified CI, explicit owner approval, version/tag and optional separate collection integration | FINAL GATE, **not** unlocked by the present draft tests |

**How to read the estimate:** F2 and F1 research may overlap, but F4 requires F3's decisions. F5 and F6 depend on reviewed implementations/evidence; F7 requires acceptance of the **selected** release scope. The estimate may expand as reviews or client testing reveal actual defects. No completion date or percentage is claimed.

## F. Every new change — checkpoint routine

1. **Before coding:** Identify its existing workstream (A–E), read previous results and GitHub threads, note any owner permission needed, and advise taking backups before touching installed/client/server data.
2. **During research:** Make smallest safe DRAFT branch/PR, preserve previous sources, use synthetic/public data only, document exact fixtures and nonmutation. Do not force-push or rewrite original PRs.
3. **At evidence:** Compare current head against `335`, check exact SHA/changed files, inspect **all GitHub workflows and logs**, record actual observed counts and unknowns; track reviewer PASS separately.
4. **After a new task:** Update **this master checklist** (workstream state + evidence + what remains), the **single CS02 queue** if peer work is needed, and the technical PR/comment; never lose older tasks.
5. **Before production:** Require explicit maintainer choices, independent exact-head review, backup/rollback plan and separate manual QA; no implied permission from a checklist checkbox.

**Current owner action required:** **none for ongoing read-only/synthetic research**. The eight Part 3 decisions and manual in-game QA will require explicit owner participation later; nothing in this document asks the maintainer to do them immediately.
